_Archived as returned by the reviewer (Martin Aasa) and applied on 2026-10-25: the Estonian sections, the store listing and the sentence openers. The workbook section is left out, since its verdicts are `prisma/data/workbook-read.json` and the refusals in `lib/dict/refused.ts`; the Russian and Ukrainian sections are left out, since their verdicts are `docs/translations/confirmed.json`. Five words in the reviewer's notes are respelled the American way, which every document here is held to. What the session changed while applying it is in `2026-10-25-written-lines-applied.md` beside this file._

# Kodukeel: every written Estonian, Russian and Ukrainian line not yet reviewed

Generated 2026-10-25 from `main` at 7dbcab8. Written text only; recordings are left for later.

How to use it: tick `[x]` when a line is right as it stands. Where it is wrong, write the fix on the line under it, starting with `FIX:`. Every entry has an id (for example `ET-J12`, `RU-4051`) so it can be applied back to the file it came from.

### A2 33. By boat and by plane (`a2-33-boat`)

- [x] **ET-L19.** lennu _(Words (form))_  
  → of a flight
- [x] **ET-L20.** laevaga, lennukiga, rongiga, sadamast, lennujaama, lennujaamasse _(How it works)_  
  → How you go is -ga: {laevaga}, {lennukiga}, {rongiga}. Where from is -st: {sadamast}. Where to is -sse, and some words have a short form too: {lennujaama} is the same as {lennujaamasse}.
- [x] **ET-L21.** Lennujaam, lennu, jaam, lennuk, lennujaama _(How it works)_  
  → {Lennujaam} is a flight station, {lennu} plus {jaam}, and {lennuk} comes from the same word. The endings go on its last part, {lennujaama}.

### A2 38. Bigger and cheaper (`a2-38-compare`)

- [x] **ET-L23.** halva _(Words (form))_  
  → of the bad one
- [x] **ET-L24.** vanem _(Words (form))_  
  → older
- [x] **ET-L25.** odava, odavam, kalli, kallim, vana, vanem _(How it works)_  
  → Most words just add -m to the [omastav] (2nd form): {odava} gives {odavam}, {kalli} gives {kallim}. A short word whose [omastav] (2nd form) ends in -a changes the -a to -e: {vana} gives {vanem}.
- [x] **ET-L26.** hea, parem, halb, halva, halvem _(How it works)_  
  → Good has its own word, like in English: {hea} and {parem}. Bad follows the rule: {halb}, {halva}, {halvem}.

  → No problem!
- [x] **ET-L43.** Otsima, vajama, Ma otsin telefoni, Leidma, Ma ostsin raamatu, ei, Ma ei leia seda _(How it works)_  
  → {Otsima} and {vajama} always take the [osastav] (3rd form): {Ma otsin telefoni}. {Leidma} takes the [omastav] (2nd form) for a whole thing found, the way {Ma ostsin raamatu} does, but after {ei} it takes the [osastav]: {Ma ei leia seda}.

## Estonian: Juku jokes

From `lib/collections/jokes.ts`. One checkbox per joke; note the line if one needs fixing.

- [x] **ET-J1.**
  - Õpetaja: Juku, nimeta viis Aafrika looma!  
    → Teacher: Juku, name five African animals!
  - Juku: Neli lõvi ja üks elevant.  
    → Juku: Four lions and one elephant.
- [x] **ET-J2.**
  - Õpetaja: Juku, kui sul on kümme õuna ja sa annad Marile viis, mis sul siis on?  
    → Teacher: Juku, if you have ten apples and you give Mari five, what do you have then?
  - Juku: Hea süda.  
    → Juku: A good heart.
- [ ] **ET-J3.**
  - Õpetaja: Juku, miks su kodutöö on isa käekirjas?  
    → Teacher: Juku, why is your homework in your dad's handwriting?
  - Juku: Ma kasutasin tema pastakat.  
    → Juku: I used his pen.  
  FIX: Õpetaja: Juku, miks su kodutöö on isa käekirjaga kirjutatud? → Teacher: Juku, why is your homework in your dad's handwriting?  
  _check: Natives say something is written "käekirjaga"; "isa käekirjas" sounds translated._
- [x] **ET-J4.**
  - Õpetaja: Juku, kus asub Tallinn?  
    → Teacher: Juku, where is Tallinn?
  - Juku: Õpiku kaheteistkümnendal leheküljel.  
    → Juku: On page twelve of the textbook.
- [x] **ET-J5.**
  - Õpetaja: Soojas asjad paisuvad, külmas tõmbuvad kokku. Juku, too näide!  
    → Teacher: Things get bigger in the warmth and smaller in the cold. Juku, give an example!
  - Juku: Suvel on päevad pikad, talvel lühikesed.  
    → Juku: In summer the days are long, in winter they are short.
- [x] **ET-J6.**
  - Õpetaja: Juku, sa hilinesid jälle!  
    → Teacher: Juku, you're late again!
  - Juku: Aga te ütlesite ise, et õppida pole kunagi liiga hilja.  
    → Juku: But you said yourself it's never too late to learn.
- [x] **ET-J7.**
  - Isa: Juku, kuidas koolis läks?  
    → Dad: Juku, how was school?
  - Juku: Nagu Titanicul.  
    → Juku: Like the Titanic.
  - Isa: Kuidas nii?  
    → Dad: How so?
  - Juku: Alguses läks kõik hästi, aga siis tuli kontrolltöö.  
    → Juku: It all went well at first, and then the test came along.
- [x] **ET-J8.**
  - Õpetaja: Juku, sinu kirjand koerast on sõna-sõnalt sama, mis su vennal.  
    → Teacher: Juku, your essay about the dog is word for word the same as your brother's.
  - Juku: Meil on ju sama koer.  
    → Juku: Well, we have the same dog.
- [x] **ET-J9.**
  - Õpetaja: Juku, kes avastas Ameerika?  
    → Teacher: Juku, who discovered America?
  - Juku: Kolumbus.  
    → Juku: Columbus.
  - Õpetaja: Õige. Ja millal?  
    → Teacher: Right. And when?
  - Juku: Kui ta sinna jõudis.  
    → Juku: When he got there.
- [x] **ET-J10.**
  - Õpetaja: Juku, mis on roheline ja hüppab?  
    → Teacher: Juku, what is green and jumps?
  - Juku: Konn.  
    → Juku: A frog.

## Estonian: picture example sentences

From `lib/collections/pictures.ts`: the model sentence under each scene of "Say what you see".

- [x] **ET-P1.** Tüdruk ja poiss söövad sünnipäevatorti. _(A birthday party)_  
  → The girl and the boy are eating birthday cake.
- [x] **ET-P2.** Päike paistab ja naine on rannas. _(A day at the beach)_  
  → The sun is shining and the woman is on the beach.
- [x] **ET-P3.** Naine ostab turul tomateid ja porgandeid. _(At the market)_  
  → The woman is buying tomatoes and carrots at the market.
- [x] **ET-P4.** Lapsed mängivad lumes. _(A winter day)_  
  → The children are playing in the snow.
- [x] **ET-P5.** Naine lõikab noaga porgandit. _(In the kitchen)_  
  → The woman is cutting a carrot with a knife.
- [x] **ET-P6.** Mees ootab jaamas rongi. _(At the station)_  
  → The man is waiting for the train at the station.
- [x] **ET-P7.** Hobune ja lehm on karjamaal. _(On the farm)_  
  → The horse and the cow are in the pasture.
- [x] **ET-P8.** Tüdruk kirjutab vihikusse. _(In the classroom)_  
  → The girl is writing in a notebook.
- [x] **ET-P9.** Karu ja hirv on metsas. _(In the forest)_  
  → The bear and the deer are in the forest.
- [x] **ET-P10.** Naine joob kohvi ja sööb kooki. _(At a café)_  
  → The woman is drinking coffee and eating cake.
- [x] **ET-P11.** Sajab vihma ja naine kannab mantlit. _(A rainy day in town)_  
  → It is raining and the woman is wearing a coat.
- [x] **ET-P12.** Kass istub puu all. _(In the garden)_  
  → The cat is sitting under the tree.
- [x] **ET-P13.** Lehm istub saunas ja joob mahla. _(A cow at the sauna)_  
  → The cow is sitting in the sauna, drinking juice.
- [x] **ET-P14.** Kass sõidab autoga ja koer jookseb talle järele. _(A cat at the wheel)_  
  → The cat is driving the car and the dog is running after it.
- [x] **ET-P15.** Koer töötab arvutiga ja joob kohvi. _(A dog at the office)_  
  → The dog is working on the computer and drinking coffee.
- [x] **ET-P16.** Karu ostab poest mett ja saia. _(A bear doing the shopping)_  
  → The bear is buying honey and bread at the shop.

From `lib/copy/i18n/areas/et/`. English the page says, then the Estonian draft.


### Landing page

- [x] **ET-W1.** Say it instead  
  → Ütle hoopis välja

  → Ütle üks neist lausetest
- [ ] **ET-W3.** Heard “{text}”. It’s said in a moment, unless you touch the screen.  
  → Kuulsin: „{text}“. See öeldakse kohe ära, kui sa ekraani ei puuduta.  
  FIX: Kuulsin: „{text}“. Kui sa ekraani ei puuduta, läheb see kohe edasi.  
  _check: "See öeldakse kohe ära" is clumsy; put the condition first and say the line goes ahead._
- [x] **ET-W4.** Heard “{text}”. Press it to say it.  
  → Kuulsin: „{text}“. Vajuta, et see välja öelda.
- [x] **ET-W5.** To hear you, Kodukeel needs a one-time download of {mb} MB. It stays in this browser, and what you say never leaves your device.  
  → Et sind kuulda, peab Kodukeel ühe korra alla laadima {mb} MB. See jääb sellesse brauserisse ja see, mida ütled, ei lahku kunagi sinu seadmest.
- [x] **ET-W6.** The microphone is blocked for this site, so picking a line is the way on.  
  → Mikrofon on sellel saidil blokeeritud, nii et edasi saab lause valides.
- [x] **ET-W7.** This device is too slow to hear speech, so picking a line is the way on.  
  → See seade on kõne kuulamiseks liiga aeglane, nii et edasi saab lause valides.

  FIX: Kuulsin: „{text}“, aga see ei ole ühelegi neist lausetest piisavalt sarnane. Proovi uuesti või vali üks.

- [ ] **ET-W42.** Fourteen cases is the number that makes people give up on Estonian. In practice you learn three forms of a word, sometimes four, and the rest are the same endings glued on, for every word in the language. When a word breaks the pattern, you’ll see what Estonians actually say right beside what the rule predicts. Press an ending and build one yourself.  
  → Neliteist käänet on see arv, mille peale inimesed eesti keelest loobuvad. Tegelikult õpid sõnast kolm vormi, mõnikord neli, ja ülejäänud on samad lõpud, mis liimitakse otsa, keele iga sõna puhul. Kui sõna mustrist kõrvale kaldub, näed kohe kõrval, mida eestlased tegelikult ütlevad, ja mida reegel ennustaks. Vajuta lõpule ja ehita ise üks vorm.  
  FIX: Neliteist käänet on see arv, mille peale inimesed eesti keelest loobuvad. Tegelikult õpid sõnast kolm vormi, mõnikord neli, ja ülejäänud saad, kui liimid otsa samad lõpud. Nii käib see keele iga sõnaga. Kui sõna mustrist kõrvale kaldub, näed reegli pakutud vormi kõrval, mida eestlased päriselt ütlevad. Vajuta lõpule ja ehita ise üks vorm.  
  _sure: The glued-endings clause is clumsy, and "näed kohe kõrval, mida eestlased ütlevad, ja mida reegel ennustaks" is garbled with a wrong comma._

- [x] **ET-W44.** Learn {n}  
  → Õpi {n}

- [ ] **ET-W121.** Not at all. It runs from A1 to C1, and the bits that trip up even advanced learners get extra practice: letters that change in the middle of a word, the case each verb insists on, and when an object takes which ending. Not sure where you are? Take the level check. There are mock state exam papers at A2, B1, B2 and C1 too, built fresh from real sentences and marked by clear rules rather than a model. The one exception is the spoken part, which you mark yourself.  
  → Üldsegi mitte. See ulatub A1-st C1-ni ja need kohad, mis ajavad sassi ka edasijõudnud õppijad, saavad lisaharjutust: tähed, mis muutuvad sõna keskel, kääne, mida iga tegusõna nõuab, ja see, millal saab sihitis millise lõpu. Ei tea, kus oled? Tee tasemetest. Olemas on ka proovieksamid tasemetel A2, B1, B2 ja C1, mis koostatakse iga kord päris lausetest ja mida hindavad selged reeglid, mitte mudel. Ainus erand on suuline osa, mida hindad ise.  
  FIX: Üldsegi mitte. See ulatub A1-st C1-ni ja need kohad, mis ajavad segadusse ka edasijõudnud õppijaid, saavad lisaharjutust: tähed, mis muutuvad sõna keskel, kääne, mida iga tegusõna nõuab, ja see, millal saab sihitis millise lõpu. - [x] **ET-W122.** Will it actually get me talking to people?  
  → Kas see paneb mind päriselt inimestega rääkima?
- [ ] **ET-W123.** Yes. You'll practice with people who want something from you: a receptionist, a landlord, a clerk. What you say is checked against the dictionary, never graded by an AI, so you can't be told you were wrong when you were right. Every morning the app asks whether you spoke Estonian to anyone yesterday, and if not, it gives you one small thing to say out loud. It counts those conversations, even the ones where somebody switched to English. It can hear Estonian too: say a line instead of typing it, and it works out which words you said on your own device, so the sound never leaves it. It won't score how a word sounded: a recognizer quietly corrects most slips into the right word, so it can't tell you that.  
  → Jah. Harjutad inimestega, kes tahavad sinult midagi: registraatori, üürileandja, ametnikuga. Seda, mida ütled, kontrollitakse sõnaraamatu järgi, mitte ei hinda tehisintellekt, nii et sulle ei saa öelda, et eksisid, kui sul oli õigus. Igal hommikul küsib rakendus, kas rääkisid eile kellegagi eesti keeles, ja kui ei, annab sulle ühe väikese asja, mida valjusti öelda. Ta loeb need vestlused kokku, ka need, kus keegi läks üle inglise keelele. Ta kuuleb ka eesti keelt: ütle lause trükkimise asemel välja ja ta tuvastab sinu enda seadmes, milliseid sõnu ütlesid, nii et heli ei lahku sealt. Seda, kuidas sõna kõlas, ta ei hinda: kõnetuvastus parandab enamiku väikeseid vigu vaikselt õigeks sõnaks, nii et ta ei saa sulle seda öelda.  
  FIX: Jah. Harjutad inimestega, kes tahavad sinult midagi, näiteks registraatori, üürileandja või ametnikuga. Seda, mida ütled, kontrollitakse sõnaraamatu järgi, mitte ei hinda tehisintellekt, nii et sulle ei saa öelda, et eksisid, kui sul oli õigus. Igal hommikul küsib rakendus, kas rääkisid eile kellegagi eesti keeles, ja kui mitte, annab sulle ühe väikese asja, mida valjusti öelda. Ta loeb need vestlused kokku, ka need, kus keegi läks üle inglise keelele. Ta kuuleb ka eesti keelt: ütle lause trükkimise asemel välja ja ta tuvastab sinu enda seadmes, milliseid sõnu ütlesid, nii et heli ei lahku sealt. Seda, kuidas sõna kõlas, ta ei hinda: kõnetuvastus parandab enamiku väikestest vigadest vaikselt õigeks sõnaks, nii et ta ei saa sulle seda öelda.  
  _sure: The elided comitative list needs "või", "enamiku väikeseid vigu" should be "enamiku väikestest vigadest", and "kui mitte" is the right form._

- [ ] **ET-W262.** **What we have sent you.** If this installation sends email, we keep a line for each message: which kind it was and when it went, so that the next one is not a repeat and so that there is a cap on how often you hear from us. We do **not** keep the message itself, and there is no tracking pixel in any of them, so nothing anywhere records whether you opened one. Every message carries a link that switches its kind off without signing in, and Settings has the same switches. The messages you cannot switch off are the ones you asked for, like a sign-in link.  
  → **Mida me oleme teile saatnud.** Kui see paigaldus saadab e-kirju, hoiame iga kirja kohta ühe rea: mis liiki kiri see oli ja millal see läks, et järgmine ei kordaks eelmist ja et kirjade sagedusel oleks piir. Kirja ennast me **ei** hoia ning üheski kirjas pole jälgimispikslit, nii et kuskil ei salvestata, kas te kirja avasite. Igas kirjas on link, mis lülitab selle liigi kirjad ilma sisselogimiseta välja, ja samad lülitid on seadetes. Välja ei saa lülitada ainult kirju, mida olete ise küsinud, näiteks sisselogimislinki.

Välja ei saa lülitada ainult kirju, mida oled ise küsinud, näiteks sisselogimislinki.

That note carries how many people practiced and how many answers they gave between them, and never a name, a figure about one person, or anybody’s mistakes. Those are on the board inside the app, where everyone who joined was told they would be. It is switched off from the group’s own page.

- [ ] **ET-W290.** None of that is a tracker and none of it is shared with anybody. Estonian law requires your agreement before something is stored on your device unless it is strictly necessary for the service you asked for, and each of these is: without them, answers you gave offline would be lost. That is why there is no cookie banner. Signing out removes the outbox, the saved session, the pages kept for offline use, any unfinished paper, any puzzle and any workbook page, so the next person on a shared computer starts from nothing; the theme and the install prompt stay, since they are about the device rather than about you. The browser also keeps a short code for which account last used it, so that a different account signing in clears the previous one's data even when nobody signed out. Clearing your browser storage removes all of it, and costs you nothing except any grade still waiting to be sent.  
  → Midagi sellest ei ole jälgija ja midagi sellest ei jagata kellegagi. - [ ] **ET-W672.** **axe over every route.** {suite} loads every page the app has, not a chosen sample, and runs axe over each one, including its best-practice rules. Two screens are the exception so far, a marked paper and a scanned page, because each needs a row made before it exists; the containment suite below makes both and measures them, and axe does not reach them yet. It runs the whole sweep again in the dark theme, because light and dark are two palettes and a color that clears the bar in one says nothing about the other. It runs it twice more at 390 pixels wide, which is a phone, because a phone here is different markup rather than the same markup narrower: the navigation rail is not drawn at all and a bar with a sheet behind it is drawn instead. That sheet is opened and swept too, since no address reaches it. It counts a one-character run of text as text, which is how a check mark measured at 2.52 against a bar of 4.5 was found. Beyond axe it asserts exactly one main landmark and one heading per screen, a page title that is not the landing page’s, and Estonian marked so a screen reader does not read it with English phonics.  
  → **axe igal marsruudil.** {suite} laadib iga rakenduse lehe, mitte valitud näidise, ja käivitab igaühel axe, sealhulgas selle parimate tavade reeglid. Siiani on erandiks kaks ekraani, hinnatud eksamitöö ja skannitud lehekülg, sest kumbki vajab enne olemasolu andmerida; allpool kirjeldatud paigutuse testikomplekt loob mõlemad ja mõõdab neid, aga axe veel nendeni ei jõua. See teeb kogu kontrolli uuesti tumedas teemas, sest hele ja tume on kaks eri paletti ja värv, mis ühes läbib, ei ütle teise kohta midagi. Ta teeb seda veel kaks korda 390 piksli laiusel, mis on telefon, sest telefon on siin teistsugune märgistus, mitte sama märgistus kitsamana: navigeerimisriba ei joonistata üldse ja selle asemel joonistatakse riba, mille taga on leht. Ka see leht avatakse ja kontrollitakse, sest ükski aadress sinna ei vii. Ta loeb ka ühe märgi pikkuse teksti tekstiks, ja nii leiti linnuke, mille kontrast oli 2,52, kui piir on 4,5. Lisaks axele kinnitab ta, et igal ekraanil on täpselt üks põhimaamärk ja üks pealkiri, et lehe pealkiri ei ole avalehe oma ja et eestikeelne tekst on märgitud, nii et ekraanilugeja ei loe seda ingliskeelse hääldusega.  
  FIX: **axe igal marsruudil.** {suite} laadib rakenduse iga lehe, mitte valitud valimit, ja käivitab igaühel axe, sealhulgas selle parimate tavade reeglid. Siiani on erandiks kaks ekraani, hinnatud eksamitöö ja skannitud lehekülg, sest kumbki tekib alles siis, kui andmebaasis on vastav rida; allpool kirjeldatud paigutuse testikomplekt loob mõlemad ja mõõdab neid, aga axe nendeni veel ei jõua. Seejärel teeb see kogu kontrolli uuesti tumedas teemas, sest hele ja tume on kaks eri paletti ja värv, mis ühes piiri ületab, ei ütle teise kohta midagi. Veel kaks korda teeb see kontrolli 390 piksli laiusel, mis vastab telefonile, sest telefonis on märgistus teistsugune, mitte lihtsalt kitsam: navigeerimisriba ei joonistata üldse ja selle asemel on riba, mille taga on avanev paneel. Ka see paneel avatakse ja kontrollitakse, sest ükski aadress sinna ei vii. See loeb ka ühemärgilise teksti tekstiks ning nii leiti linnuke, mille kontrast oli 2,52, kuigi nõutav piir on 4,5. Lisaks axele kinnitab see, et igal ekraanil on täpselt üks põhimaamärk ja üks pealkiri, et lehe pealkiri ei ole sama mis avalehel ja et eestikeelne tekst on märgitud, nii et ekraanilugeja ei loe seda ingliskeelse hääldusega.  
  _check: 'iga rakenduse lehe' means every app's page; 'enne olemasolu andmerida', 'telefon on märgistus' and 'leht' for the sheet are unclear; See/Ta mixed._

- [x] **ET-W677.** **The timed practice round can be set to run longer** (WCAG 2.2.1, Timing Adjustable).   → **Ajapiiranguga harjutusvooru saab seada kauem kestma** (WCAG 2.2.1, reguleeritav ajastus). Päevane ülesanne on alguses kaks minutit ja üks seade venitab selle kuni kümme korda pikemaks, nagu kriteerium nõuab. See valitakse enne vooru algust seadetes ja alguseekraanil on sinna link.

- [ ] **ET-W682.** **The listening, dictation and minimal-pair rounds require hearing** (WCAG 1.2.1). There is no transcript before the answer, because the transcript is the answer, so a text alternative would remove the exercise. The text is shown as soon as the answer is in. - [ ] **ET-W700.** Kodukeel sets a mock paper at A2, B1, B2 and C1, plus one of its own at A1. Each one keeps the published time limits, the points, the pass mark and the rule that you fail if any part scores zero, and every task tells you which official task it stands in for. The questions are built from sentences a lexicographer recorded, so they aren’t the Board’s own. Nothing here scores your pronunciation, and it’s free.  
  → Kodukeel pakub proovieksamit tasemetel A2, B1, B2 ja C1 ning omaenda proovieksamit tasemel A1. - [ ] **ET-W859.** The plans, the tooling, the mail and the domain come to about three hundred dollars a month before a single learner arrives, and most of that does not move when they do. So the first thousand people are nearly free to serve, and the cost per head falls by roughly a factor of ten for each decade. What grows instead is speech and the database, so past ten thousand the shape is set by how much is said aloud and how many years of reviews are being kept.  
  → Paketid, tööriistad, e-post ja domeen teevad kokku umbes kolmsada dollarit kuus enne, kui ainsatki õppijat on, ja suurem osa sellest ei muutu, kui nad tulevad. Nii et esimese tuhande inimese teenindamine on peaaegu tasuta ja kulu inimese kohta langeb iga suurusjärguga umbes kümme korda. Hoopis kasvavad kõnesüntees ja andmebaas, nii et üle kümne tuhande määrab kuju see, kui palju valjusti öeldakse ja mitme aasta kordamisi hoitakse.  
  FIX: Paketid, tööriistad, e-post ja domeen maksavad kokku umbes kolmsada dollarit kuus juba enne, kui tuleb esimene õppija, ja suurem osa sellest ei muutu ka siis, kui õppijad tulevad. Seega on esimese tuhande inimese teenindamine peaaegu tasuta ja kulu inimese kohta langeb iga suurusjärguga umbes kümme korda. Kasvavad hoopis kõnesüntees ja andmebaas, nii et üle kümne tuhande õppija puhul sõltub kuju sellest, kui palju ette loetakse ja mitme aasta kordamisi alles hoitakse.  
  _check: 'Hoopis kasvavad' word order, sentence-initial 'Nii et' and 'kui palju valjusti öeldakse' are unnatural._

## Estonian: brief for EKI (docs/45-ekilex.md, "Eesti keeles")

One checkbox per paragraph or bullet.

- [x] **ET-E1.** *Mustand, mis ootab selle rakenduse arendaja, emakeelse eesti keele kõneleja läbilugemist. Enne seda mitte saata.*

### 1. Mis on Kodukeel

- [ ] **ET-E2.** Kodukeel on tasuta veebirakendus täiskasvanutele, kes õpivad eesti keelt. Enamik neist elab Eestis ja räägib juba vene, ukraina või inglise keelt. Rakenduses on sõnastik, kursus, kordamine ja vestlusharjutused. Selle üks kindel reegel on, et rakendus ei kirjuta ise eesti keelt: iga sõnavorm ja iga näitelause, mida õppijale õpetatakse või mille järgi teda hinnatakse, tuleb Ekilexist või on lõpp, mis on lisatud Ekilexis talletatud tüvele, ja ekraan ütleb, kumb see on.  
  FIX: Kodukeel on tasuta veebirakendus täiskasvanutele, kes õpivad eesti keelt. Enamik neist elab Eestis ja räägib juba vene, ukraina või inglise keelt. Rakenduses on sõnastik, kursus, kordamine ja vestlusharjutused. Rakendusel on üks kindel reegel: sõnastikus ja harjutustes ei kirjuta see ise eesti keelt. Iga sõnavorm ja iga näitelause, mida õppijale õpetatakse või mille järgi teda hinnatakse, pärineb Ekilexist või on saadud Ekilexis talletatud tüvele lõpu lisamisel, ja ekraan näitab, kumb see on.  
  _check: 'Rakendus ei kirjuta ise eesti keelt' is broader than true (lessons and pages are the app's own Estonian), and as written the form 'is an ending'._

### 3. Kuidas rakendus küsib

- [ ] **ET-E7.** **Korraga kõige rohkem kaks päringut** iga töötava protsessi kohta, rakenduses ja igas skriptis. Skript võib kahest üle minna ainult siis, kui keegi annab lipu `--past-the-limit`, mis trükib hoiatuse.

## Estonian: twins (words easy to confuse)

From `lib/collections/twins.ts`. Check each English meaning fits its Estonian word, and the hint where there is one.

- [x] **ET-T1.** **kasvama** = to grow (by itself); **kasvatama** = to grow something, to raise
- [x] **ET-T2.** **mõjuma** = to have an effect; **mõjutama** = to influence somebody
- [x] **ET-T3.** **harjuma** = to get used to; **harjutama** = to practice
- [x] **ET-T4.** **näima** = to seem; **näitama** = to show
- [x] **ET-T5.** **säilima** = to last, to be kept; **säilitama** = to keep, to preserve
- [x] **ET-T6.** **valmima** = to ripen, to get finished; **valmistama** = to make, to prepare
- [x] **ET-T7.** **hävima** = to be destroyed; **hävitama** = to destroy
- [x] **ET-T8.** **tutvuma** = to get to know; **tutvustama** = to introduce
- [x] **ET-T9.** **põlema** = to burn (by itself); **põletama** = to burn something
- [x] **ET-T10.** **soovima** = to wish, to want; **soovitama** = to recommend
- [x] **ET-T11.** **puuduma** = to be missing; **puudutama** = to touch, to concern
- [x] **ET-T12.** **muutma** = to change something; **muutuma** = to change, to become different
- [x] **ET-T13.** **peatama** = to stop something; **peatuma** = to stop, to come to a halt
- [x] **ET-T14.** **jätkama** = to carry on with something; **jätkuma** = to go on, to last
- [x] **ET-T15.** **leidma** = to find; **leiduma** = to be found, to be there
- [x] **ET-T16.** **üllatama** = to surprise somebody; **üllatuma** = to be surprised
- [x] **ET-T17.** **huvitama** = to interest somebody; **huvituma** = to get interested
- [x] **ET-T18.** **toetama** = to support somebody; **toetuma** = to lean on, to rely on
- [x] **ET-T19.** **täitma** = to fill, to fill in; **täituma** = to fill up, to come true
- [x] **ET-T20.** **kohtama** = to run into, to come across; **kohtuma** = to meet (by arrangement). Hint: Running into somebody takes them as an object. Meeting them on purpose is meeting with them.
- [x] **ET-T21.** **tundma** = to know (a person), to feel; **tunduma** = to seem
- [x] **ET-T22.** **keelama** = to forbid; **keelduma** = to refuse
- [x] **ET-T23.** **petma** = to cheat, to deceive; **pettuma** = to be disappointed
- [x] **ET-T24.** **kuulma** = to hear; **kuulama** = to listen
- [x] **ET-T25.** **nägema** = to see; **vaatama** = to look, to watch. Hint: No shared root here, but the same split: seeing just happens, looking is on purpose.
- [x] **ET-T26.** **algama** = to begin (by itself); **alustama** = to begin something; **hakkama** = to start doing. Hint: Nothing after it: the thing begins by itself. An object after it: you begin something. Another verb after it: you start doing that.
- [x] **ET-T27.** **teadma** = to know (a fact); **tundma** = to know (a person or place), to feel. Hint: Facts and answers take the first. People, places and feelings take the second.
- [x] **ET-T28.** **minema** = to go (somewhere); **käima** = to go and come back, to go regularly. Hint: Going somewhere is a trip with a direction. The other is a round trip or a habit.
- [x] **ET-T29.** **oskama** = to know how to; **suutma** = to manage to; **võima** = may, can (it is possible); **tohtima** = to be allowed to. Hint: A skill you learned, the strength to do it, a possibility, or permission.
- [x] **ET-T30.** **jääma** = to stay, to remain; **jätma** = to leave something behind. Hint: You stay somewhere yourself. You leave something somewhere else.
- [x] **ET-T31.** **viima** = to take (away from here); **tooma** = to bring (here); **kandma** = to carry, to wear. Hint: Away from where you are, towards where you are, or simply holding it as you go.
- [x] **ET-T32.** **arvama** = to think (have an opinion); **mõtlema** = to think (in your head), to mean. Hint: An opinion takes the first. Thinking something over, or meaning something, takes the second.
- [x] **ET-T33.** **jõudma** = to get there, to manage; **saabuma** = to arrive. Hint: The first is everyday and also means managing in time. The second is the arrivals board.
- [x] **ET-T34.** **valetama** = to tell a lie; **lamama** = to lie down, to be lying. Hint: English lies in two ways. Telling a lie and lying on the sofa are two words here.
- [x] **ET-T35.** **ostma** = to buy; **otsima** = to look for
- [x] **ET-T36.** **õpetama** = to teach; **lõpetama** = to finish
- [x] **ET-T37.** **vastama** = to answer; **vaatama** = to look, to watch
- [x] **ET-T38.** **jooma** = to drink; **tooma** = to bring; **looma** = to create; **lootma** = to hope
- [x] **ET-T39.** **võtma** = to take; **võima** = may, can; **võitma** = to win; **viima** = to take away
- [x] **ET-T40.** **saama** = to get, to become; **saatma** = to send; **sadama** = to rain, to snow
- [x] **ET-T41.** **sööma** = to eat; **lööma** = to hit
- [x] **ET-T42.** **ärkama** = to wake up; **märkama** = to notice
- [x] **ET-T43.** **nõudma** = to demand; **jõudma** = to get there, to manage
- [x] **ET-T44.** **sõitma** = to drive, to ride; **võitma** = to win
- [x] **ET-T45.** **kuulama** = to listen; **kuuluma** = to belong
- [x] **ET-T46.** **juhtuma** = to happen; **juhtima** = to lead, to drive
- [x] **ET-T47.** **valetama** = to tell a lie; **valutama** = to ache; **vahetama** = to swap, to change
- [x] **ET-T48.** **valima** = to choose, to vote; **valama** = to pour
- [x] **ET-T49.** **aitama** = to help; **eitama** = to deny
- [x] **ET-T50.** **karistama** = to punish; **koristama** = to tidy up
- [x] **ET-T51.** **kaebama** = to complain; **kaevama** = to dig
- [x] **ET-T52.** **kaitsma** = to protect; **maitsma** = to taste
- [x] **ET-T53.** **helistama** = to phone; **eelistama** = to prefer
- [x] **ET-T54.** **küsima** = to ask; **püsima** = to stay put, to last
- [x] **ET-T55.** **kutsuma** = to invite, to call; **katsuma** = to touch
- [x] **ET-T56.** **arutama** = to discuss; **arvutama** = to calculate
- [x] **ET-T57.** **märkima** = to mark, to note down; **märkama** = to notice
- [x] **ET-T58.** **juuni** = June; **juuli** = July
- [x] **ET-T59.** **keel** = language, tongue; **kell** = clock, o'clock
- [x] **ET-T60.** **koht** = place; **kõht** = stomach; **kott** = bag
- [x] **ET-T61.** **linn** = town, city; **lind** = bird
- [x] **ET-T62.** **pilt** = picture; **pilet** = ticket
- [x] **ET-T63.** **kala** = fish; **kana** = chicken
- [x] **ET-T64.** **laud** = table; **laul** = song
- [x] **ET-T65.** **köök** = kitchen; **söök** = food, a meal
- [x] **ET-T66.** **vale** = wrong; **valge** = white
- [x] **ET-T67.** **halb** = bad; **hall** = gray
- [x] **ET-T68.** **kerge** = light, easy; **kõrge** = high, tall
- [x] **ET-T69.** **kurb** = sad; **kurt** = deaf
- [x] **ET-T70.** **vihane** = angry; **vihmane** = rainy
- [x] **ET-T71.** **raske** = heavy, difficult; **rase** = pregnant

## Estonian: grammar page example sentences

From `lib/estonian/grammarExamples.ts`. Does the sentence show the point above it? The marked word is in bold.


### Topic: olema

_The verb you'll use in every conversation, and it's irregular_

- [x] **ET-G1.** **Olen** natuke kurb.
- [x] **ET-G2.** Nad **on** õde ja vend.

_The owner gets -l, which already says "has". "Is" always follows_

- [x] **ET-G3.** **Mul** on oma maja.
- [x] **ET-G4.** **Tal** on suur perekond.

_Being sleepy or hungry, or having to do something, works the same way_

- [x] **ET-G5.** **Mul** on kange uni.
- [x] **ET-G6.** **Tal** on hea isu.


### Topic: present-tense

_One ending each for I, you, he or she, we, you all and they_

- [x] **ET-G7.** **Alustame** tööd esmaspäeval.
- [x] **ET-G8.** Mida te nädalavahetusel **teete**?

_One form for both "I eat" and "I'm eating"_

- [x] **ET-G9.** Ahv **sööb** banaani.
- [x] **ET-G10.** Karu **magab** talveund.

_It covers the future too, as in "the concert is tomorrow"_

- [x] **ET-G11.** Kontsert **toimub** homme.
- [x] **ET-G12.** Kahe riigi esindajad **kohtuvad** homme.


### Topic: negation

_The verb loses its ending and goes back to its shortest shape_

- [x] **ET-G13.** Aeg ei **peatu**.
- [x] **ET-G14.** Ma ei **leia** oma rahakotti.

_The same "not" for everyone, where English juggles don't and doesn't_

- [x] **ET-G15.** Mulle **ei** meeldi juuksuris käia.
- [x] **ET-G16.** Ta **ei** söö rasvast sealiha.

_The past says no in a different way from the present_

- [x] **ET-G17.** Isa ja ema ei **olnud** kodus.
- [x] **ET-G18.** Koha valik **polnud** juhuslik.


### Topic: imperfect

_"I did" usually ends in -sin, and some common verbs take -in_

- [x] **ET-G19.** **Käisin** meres ujumas.
- [x] **ET-G20.** **Eksisin** metsa ära.

_Some very common verbs use -i- instead of -si-_

- [x] **ET-G21.** **Jõin** klaasi vett.
- [ ] **ET-G22.** Kala **ujus** sügavamale.  
  FIX: (replace: 'ujus' is third person -s and ujuma is a -si- verb (ujusin), so it does not show the -i- past at all. Suggestion: 'Tõin poest piima.' (bold Tõin) or 'Sõin hommikul putru.' (bold Sõin).)  
  _sure_

_For anything that's over, even if it was five minutes ago_

- [x] **ET-G23.** **Jõin** tassi kohvi.
- [x] **ET-G24.** **Ootasime** kontserdi algust.


### Topic: perfect

_Built with "to be", never with "to have"_

- [x] **ET-G25.** Mare **on** aastaid kiirabis töötanud.
- [x] **ET-G26.** Riigi elanike arv **on** kasvanud.

_For when the result matters more than when it happened_

- [x] **ET-G27.** Värviline sügis on kätte **jõudnud**.
- [x] **ET-G28.** Käärid on nüriks **läinud**.

_The -nud word stays the same for every person_

- [x] **ET-G29.** Ta on **saanud** eestikeelse hariduse.
- [x] **ET-G30.** Laulupeod on Eestis traditsiooniks **saanud**.


### Topic: pluperfect

_Something that was over before something else happened_

- [x] **ET-G31.** Näkku **oli** tekkinud uus vistrik.
- [ ] **ET-G32.** Kohale **olid** tulnud tähtsad vaimulikud isandad.  
  FIX: (replace: 'tähtsad vaimulikud isandad' (important clerical gentlemen) is old-fashioned and odd for a learner. Suggestion: 'Kui me kohale jõudsime, oli film juba alanud.' (bold oli).)  
  _check_

_Common in stories, and when you retell what someone said_

- [x] **ET-G33.** Nikolai **oli** läinud hommikul metsa seenele ja polnud õhtuks tagasi jõudnud.
- [ ] **ET-G34.** P**oli**tsei oli saanud mitu vihjet võimalike süüdlaste kohta.  
  FIX: (replace: the bold is broken, it marks 'oli' inside the word 'Politsei' (P**oli**tsei) instead of the auxiliary 'oli'. Fix the markup to 'Politsei **oli** saanud mitu vihjet võimalike süüdlaste kohta.')  
  _sure_

_Uses exactly the same -nud word as "have done"_

- [x] **ET-G35.** Risuhunnik oli nõgestesse **kasvanud**.
- [x] **ET-G36.** Riigi elanike arv on **kasvanud**.


### Topic: conditional

_Wishes and what-ifs: "if I had time, I'd come"_

- [x] **ET-G37.** Hea, kui elukoht **asuks** töökoha lähedal.
- [ ] **ET-G38.** Kui **oleksin** maal edasi, künnaksin traktoriga põldu.  
  FIX: (replace: 'Kui oleksin maal edasi' is an awkward, unclear phrasing and plowing with a tractor is an odd scenario for a learner. Suggestion: 'Kui mul oleks aega, tuleksin kaasa.' (bold oleks or tuleksin), which also matches the context's own example.)  
  _check_

_Asking nicely: "I'd like a coffee", rather than "I want"_

- [x] **ET-G39.** Ma **tahaksin** sinult midagi paluda.
- [x] **ET-G40.** **Tahaksin** vana külmiku tasuta ära anda.

_Giving advice without bossing anyone around_

- [x] **ET-G41.** Kui hammas valutab, siis **peaksid** hambaarsti juurde minema.
- [x] **ET-G42.** Neid probleeme **tuleks** koosolekul arutada.


### Topic: imperative

_One form for one person, another for a group, unlike English_

- [x] **ET-G43.** **Istu** minu kõrvale.
- [x] **ET-G44.** **Peske** käed puhtaks.

_The group form is also the polite one for a single stranger_

- [x] **ET-G45.** Palun **öelge**, kus siin tualett on.
- [x] **ET-G46.** **Saatke** mulle takso.

_"Don't" has a little word of its own_

- [x] **ET-G47.** **Ära** otse päikesesse vaata!
- [x] **ET-G48.** **Ära** muretse, kõik läheb hästi.


### Topic: impersonal

_Signs, instructions, forms and the news_

- [x] **ET-G49.** Dessertveini **serveeritakse** koos magustoiduga.
- [x] **ET-G50.** Doonoril **kontrollitakse** vererõhku.

_Says people did something, without saying which people_

- [x] **ET-G51.** Kuidas seda asutust **nimetatakse**?
- [x] **ET-G52.** Ratsutamist **peetakse** kulukaks hobiks.


### Topic: past-participle

_With "to be" it makes "have done" and "had done"_

- [x] **ET-G53.** Oled **elanud** vales.
- [x] **ET-G54.** Mulle on alati **meeldinud** tantsida.

_Describes something by what it has done, like "a fallen leaf"_

- [x] **ET-G55.** **Ilmunud** vihik tegi luuletajale palju rõõmu.
- [x] **ET-G56.** **Kukkunud** vanainimene vajas kiirabi.

_Has a twin in -tud for things done to something_

- [x] **ET-G57.** Vanaema **küpsetatud** kook.
- [x] **ET-G58.** Laste **joonistatud** pildid.


### Topic: participles

_They describe a noun, the way "a sleeping baby" does_

- [x] **ET-G59.** Mobiiltelefoniga **tehtud** fotod.
- [x] **ET-G60.** Masinal **kootud** vaip.

_With "to be", they make "has done" and "had done"_

- [x] **ET-G61.** Riigi elanike arv on **kasvanud**.
- [x] **ET-G62.** Näkku oli **tekkinud** uus vistrik.


### Topic: politeness

_Calling one stranger "you" in the plural, to be polite_

- [x] **ET-G63.** Kas te **soovite** teed või kohvi?
- [x] **ET-G64.** Kas te **maksate** sularahas või ülekandega?

_The "would" form, to make a request gentler_

- [x] **ET-G65.** Ma **tahaksin** sinult midagi paluda.
- [x] **ET-G66.** **Tahaksin** vana külmiku tasuta ära anda.

_Being direct is less rude here than English speakers expect_

- [x] **ET-G67.** Palun **öelge**, kus siin tualett on.
- [x] **ET-G68.** **Saatke** mulle takso.


### Topic: time-expressions

_Days, seasons and years take -l, and months take -s_

- [x] **ET-G69.** Koosolek toimub **teisipäeval**.
- [x] **ET-G70.** Lähen **juulis** puhkusele.

_"For how long" works differently again_

- [x] **ET-G71.** Kirurg tegi päeva **jooksul** kolm operatsiooni.
- [x] **ET-G72.** Film kestis kaks **tundi**.

_From and until each have their own ending_

- [x] **ET-G73.** Käin tööl **esmaspäevast** reedeni.
- [x] **ET-G74.** Pääsuke laulab aprillist **augustini**.


### Topic: aspect

_Done, and all of it: "I ate the cake", the whole cake_

- [x] **ET-G75.** Ostsin odava **auto**.
- [x] **ET-G76.** Sõin **taldriku** tühjaks.

_Unfinished, or only some: the "some of it" ending_

- [x] **ET-G77.** Sõin tüki **torti**.
- [x] **ET-G78.** Jõin klaasi **vett**.

_Small words like "up" and "off" underline that it's finished_

- [x] **ET-G79.** Palun koori kartulid **ära**.
- [x] **ET-G80.** Hambapasta sai **otsa**.


### Topic: infinitives

_The -ma one comes after "start", "go" and "have to"_

- [x] **ET-G81.** Ema hakkab kooki **küpsetama**.
- [x] **ET-G82.** Orkester hakkas **mängima**.

_The -da one comes after "want" and "can"_

- [x] **ET-G83.** Kus saab valuutat **vahetada**?
- [ ] **ET-G84.** Haige võib varsti **surra**.  
  FIX: (replace: 'The sick person may soon die' is grim for a learner page and surra is an irregular -da form that can confuse. Suggestion: 'Kas ma võin siia istuda?' (bold istuda) or 'Ma tahan eesti keelt õppida.' (bold õppida).)  
  _check_


### Topic: particle-verbs

_The small word usually adds "all the way" or a direction_

- [x] **ET-G85.** Lülita veekeetja **sisse**.
- [x] **ET-G86.** Tõmba uks **kinni**.

_Often it's the difference between doing and getting it done_

- [x] **ET-G87.** Lamp põles **läbi**.
- [x] **ET-G88.** Etenduse piletid osteti kiiresti **ära**.

_It wanders around the sentence instead of staying by the verb_

- [x] **ET-G89.** Pane televiisor **kinni**.
- [x] **ET-G90.** Keerasin kraani **kinni**.


### Topic: converb

_Two things at once, with no "and" or "while" needed_

- [ ] **ET-G91.** **Naerdes** paljastusid laiad esihambad ja igemed.  
  FIX: (replace: dangling converb, grammatically the teeth and gums are doing the laughing, which contradicts the rule taught in this same topic (the converb's doer is the sentence's subject). Suggestion: 'Ta rääkis naerdes.' or 'Isa luges ajalehte kohvi juues.' (bold the -des word).)  
  _sure_
- [ ] **ET-G92.** Mees köhib tatti **pritsides**.  
  FIX: (replace: 'coughs spraying snot' is unpleasant to show a learner. Suggestion: 'Ema laulis süüa tehes.' (bold tehes).)  
  _check_

_Writers much prefer it to two sentences joined up_

- [ ] **ET-G93.** Peo **edenedes** pidurid kadusid.  
  FIX: (replace: word order breaks verb-second (natural is 'Peo edenedes kadusid pidurid'), 'pidurid kadusid' is an idiom (inhibitions went) a learner will read literally, and the converb's doer differs from the subject, against the rule taught in this topic. Suggestion: 'Koju jõudes panin kohe veekeetja tööle.' (bold jõudes).)  
  _sure_
- [x] **ET-G94.** Purustuste järgi **otsustades** võis tegu olla keeristormiga.

_Whoever does it is whoever does the rest of the sentence_

- [x] **ET-G95.** Ta kuulis juhtunust puhkusel **viibides**.
- [x] **ET-G96.** Ta oli auhinnast **kuuldes** rõõmsalt üllatunud.


### Topic: quotative

_Rumors, hearsay and things you read somewhere_

- [x] **ET-G97.** Tammetõrude rohkus **ennustavat** karmi talve.
- [x] **ET-G98.** Aus ülestunnistus **pidavat** karistust kergendama.

_Can sound doubtful, depending on how it's said_

- [x] **ET-G99.** Mees väidab end mitte **teadvat**, kuhu ta auto jättis.
- [x] **ET-G100.** Kohus andis hagi tagasi, sest see **olevat** puudulikult koostatud.


### Topic: numerals

_From two up, the noun takes the singular "some of it" form_

- [x] **ET-G101.** Aias kasvab kolm **pirni**.
- [x] **ET-G102.** Ostsin kaks **pudelit** vett.

_Numbers take endings too, when the noun after them does_

- [x] **ET-G103.** Buss tuleb **viie** minuti pärast.
- [x] **ET-G104.** Broneerisin laua **kahele**.

_First, second and so on are regular, and take endings too_

- [x] **ET-G105.** Hotelli baar asub **esimesel** korrusel.
- [x] **ET-G106.** Laps sõidab lennukiga **esimest** korda.


### Topic: derivation

_Any verb gives a noun for doing it, like "reading"_

- [x] **ET-G107.** **Vabandamine** on talle võõras.
- [x] **ET-G108.** **Suitsetamine** on kahjulik harjumus.

_Adjectives meaning "-ish" and "-less"_

- [x] **ET-G109.** Kõlas **arglik** koputus.
- [ ] **ET-G110.** Abikaasad on **võrdõiguslikud**.  
  FIX: (replace: 'võrdõiguslik' shows neither -ish nor -less (it is -lik in a compound meaning 'having equal rights'), so the -less half of the point is not shown anywhere. Suggestion: a -tu word, e.g. 'See supp on maitsetu.' (bold maitsetu).)  
  _check_

_An adjective gives a noun for the quality, like "kindness"_

- [x] **ET-G111.** Meid seob ammune **sõprus**.
- [x] **ET-G112.** Ega **rikkus** pole häbiasi.


### Topic: nominalization

_The doer goes in front, in the form that means "whose"_

- [x] **ET-G113.** **Tarbijate** käitumine on enamasti ennustatav.
- [x] **ET-G114.** **Alliansi** laienemine on olnud edukas.


### Topic: word-order

_Endings show who did what, so the order is free to show emphasis_

- [x] **ET-G115.** **Talvel** sadas palju lund.
- [x] **ET-G116.** **Varahommikul** toimus raske liiklusõnnetus.

_The verb usually comes second_

- [x] **ET-G117.** Homme **alustan** dieeti.
- [x] **ET-G118.** Õhtul **kütan** sauna.


### Topic: idiom

_Sayings still in daily use_

- [x] **ET-G119.** Ilu peitub vaataja **silmades**.
- [x] **ET-G120.** Lähtusin põhimõttest, et topelt ei **kärise**.

_Fixed phrases that don't mean what their words say_

- [x] **ET-G121.** Häid töölisi otsitakse **tikutulega** taga.
- [x] **ET-G122.** Olime kõik naerust **kõveras**.

_Ordinary words used in a picture sense_

- [x] **ET-G123.** Suvi on **käes**.
- [x] **ET-G124.** Hambapasta sai **otsa**.


### Topic: adjective-agreement

_Same ending and same number, one or many, as its noun_

- [x] **ET-G125.** Ostsin turult **värsket** liha.
- [x] **ET-G126.** Ta ei söö **rasvast** sealiha.

_With -ni, -na, -ta and -ga, only the noun gets the full ending_

- [x] **ET-G127.** **Aeglase** vooluga jõgi.
- [x] **ET-G128.** **Õnneliku** lõpuga film.


### Topic: comparative

_Add -m to the form meaning "of", the one the endings go on_

- [ ] **ET-G129.** Anna **väiksem** lusikas!  
  FIX: (replace: 'väiksem' does not follow the stated rule (genitive väikese + m would give 'väikesem'), so it confuses the point. Suggestion: 'Täna on soojem kui eile.' (soe, sooja → soojem) or 'Anna suurem lusikas!' (suur, suure → suurem).)  
  _check_
- [x] **ET-G130.** Eesti keskmine palk on **suurem** kui Lätis.

_Either "than" plus the plain word, or no "than" and -st_

- [x] **ET-G131.** Bensiin on kallim **kui** diisel.
- [x] **ET-G132.** Ta on oma **abikaasast** oluliselt noorem.

_A few common ones are irregular, like English "good, better"_

- [x] **ET-G133.** Kumb auto on **parem**?
- [x] **ET-G134.** Täna on enesetunne palju **parem**.


### Topic: superlative

_A helper word in front of "bigger", which always works_

- [x] **ET-G135.** Veebruar on tavaliselt aasta **kõige** külmem kuu.
- [x] **ET-G136.** Sa oled mu ingel, mu **kõige** kallim!

_A one-word form, shorter and more literary_

- [x] **ET-G137.** Tallinn on Eesti **suurim** linn.
- [x] **ET-G138.** Kaisa on minu **parim** sõbranna.


### Topic: future

_A word like "tomorrow" is what makes it the future_

- [x] **ET-G139.** Kontsert toimub **homme**.
- [x] **ET-G140.** Nendest saab **varsti** abielupaar.

_Verbs like "plan" and "intend" do the rest_

- [x] **ET-G141.** Ema **hakkab** kooki küpsetama.
- [x] **ET-G142.** Valitsus **kavatseb** eelarvekulud tõsise kontrolli alla võtta.

_A small extra word can say it's going to get finished_

- [x] **ET-G143.** Autol hakkab kütus **otsa** saama.
- [x] **ET-G144.** Pastakas hakkab **tühjaks** saama.


### Topic: object

_Done, and all of it: the plain word, or the form meaning "whose"_

- [x] **ET-G145.** Ostsin odava **auto**.
- [x] **ET-G146.** Pane **raamat** lauale.

_Unfinished, or only part of it: the "some of it" ending_

- [x] **ET-G147.** Jõin klaasi **vett**.
- [x] **ET-G148.** Ahv sööb **banaani**.

_After "not": always the "some of it" ending_

- [x] **ET-G149.** Ma ei loe **ulmet**.
- [x] **ET-G150.** Ta ei söö rasvast **sealiha**.


### Topic: reported-speech

_"She said that..." plus a clause, much like English_

- [ ] **ET-G151.** Imelik, **et** teda kodus pole.  
  FIX: (replace: 'Imelik, et teda kodus pole' is not reported speech (no one says anything), so it does not show 'She said that...'. Suggestion: 'Mari ütles, et ta tuleb hiljem.' (bold et).)  
  _check_
- [x] **ET-G152.** Hotelli administraator ütles, **et** vabu tube ei ole.

_Or the -vat form, which needs no "said" at all_

- [x] **ET-G153.** Tammetõrude rohkus **ennustavat** karmi talve.
- [ ] **ET-G154.** Hiinlased **olevat** tuntud oma praktilise meele poolest.  
  FIX: (replace: a generalization about an ethnic group (the Chinese are known for their practical mind) is not a good example to show. Suggestion: 'Homme olevat ilus ilm.' (bold olevat).)  
  _check_


### Topic: concession

_Words like "even so" that start a new sentence_

- [x] **ET-G155.** Esinemine oli kokkuvõttes **siiski** rahuldav.
- [x] **ET-G156.** Mari kõhkles hetke, **ent** siis otsustas teistega kaasa minna.


### Topic: hedging

_Words like "probably", "likely" and "perhaps"_

- [x] **ET-G157.** Ema **vist** magab juba.
- [x] **ET-G158.** Hakkab **vist** sadama.

_The -vat form, which puts the claim on somebody else_

- [x] **ET-G159.** Aus ülestunnistus **pidavat** karistust kergendama.
- [x] **ET-G160.** Rooibos **leevendavat** mitmeid terviseprobleeme, näiteks unetust, pingeid ja peavalu.

_The "would" form, which softens a claim as well as a request_

- [x] **ET-G161.** Hea, kui elukoht **asuks** töökoha lähedal.
- [x] **ET-G162.** Neid probleeme **tuleks** koosolekul arutada.


### Topic: cohesion

_Contrast and result: "but", "so", "therefore"_

- [x] **ET-G163.** Kurb, **aga** tõsi.
- [x] **ET-G164.** Müristab, **aga** välku ei löö.

_Ordering and adding: first, also, finally_

- [x] **ET-G165.** Patukotist sai **lõpuks** pühak.
- [ ] **ET-G166.** Leiliruumi lagi ja seinad, **samuti** lava on lehtpuust.  
  FIX: (replace: the inserted 'samuti lava' should be closed with a comma ('..., samuti lava, on lehtpuust'), and sauna-construction vocabulary (leiliruum, lava, lehtpuu) is heavy for the point. Suggestion: 'Ostsin leiba ja piima, samuti natuke juustu.' (bold samuti).)  
  _check_

_Pointing back to something without repeating it_

- [x] **ET-G167.** Kiikusin toolil ja **see** läks katki.
- [x] **ET-G168.** Ära usu Jaani, **ta** petab.


### Topic: emphasis

_Small words that point at what matters most_

- [x] **ET-G169.** Kuulake mind **ka**!
- [x] **ET-G170.** Määri leivale võid **ka**.


### Topic: rhetorical-questions

_A small word at the start marks a real yes or no question_

- [x] **ET-G171.** **Kas** te soovite teed või kohvi?
- [x] **ET-G172.** **Kas** täna vanni teeme?


### Topic: subordination

_The word order changes inside that part_

- [ ] **ET-G173.** Töötan firmas, **mis** toodab autode varuosi.  
  FIX: (replace: the bold is on 'mis' and the clause keeps normal order ('mis toodab autode varuosi'), so it does not show the word-order change the context describes (G174 does). Suggestion: 'Ma ei tea, kus ta praegu elab.' (bold elab).)  
  _check_
- [x] **ET-G174.** Sa oled väga tubli, et oma tervisest **hoolid**.


### Topic: relative-clause

_Always separated by a comma_

- [x] **ET-G175.** Metsas on kohti, **kus** puud ei kasva.
- [x] **ET-G176.** Töötan ettevõttes, **mis** tegeleb autode remondiga.

_Different pronouns for people and for things_

- [x] **ET-G177.** Kusagil on keegi, **kes** sinust tõeliselt hoolib.
- [x] **ET-G178.** Bioloogia on teadus, **mis** uurib elu.

_Its ending depends on its job in its own part of the sentence_

- [x] **ET-G179.** Ta on hea režissöör, **kelle** filme tasub vaadata.
- [x] **ET-G180.** Tegin nimekirja asjadest, **mida** poest tuua.


### Topic: government

_Which ending comes after it is part of learning the verb_

- [x] **ET-G181.** Ta usub **Jumalasse**.
- [x] **ET-G182.** Laps mängib **palliga**.

_Helping, calling, liking and thinking are the traps_

- [x] **ET-G183.** **Mulle** ei meeldi juuksuris käia.
- [x] **ET-G184.** **Millest** sa mõtled?


### Case: NOMINATIVE

_Whoever or whatever is doing the action_

- [x] **ET-G185.** **Ahv** sööb banaani.
- [x] **ET-G186.** **Karu** magab talveund.

_A whole object after a command, or in the plural_

- [x] **ET-G187.** Pane **raamat** lauale.
- [x] **ET-G188.** Palun koori **kartulid** ära.


### Case: GENITIVE

_Saying whose something is_

- [x] **ET-G189.** Uue **filmi** esilinastus.
- [x] **ET-G190.** Rahulolev klient on iga **firma** unistus.

_A whole object, like the car you bought_

- [x] **ET-G191.** Ostsin odava **auto**.
- [x] **ET-G192.** Sõin **taldriku** tühjaks.


### Case: PARTITIVE

_Some of a thing rather than all of it_

- [x] **ET-G193.** Jõin klaasi **vett**.
- [x] **ET-G194.** Sõin tüki **torti**.

_An action you're still in the middle of_

- [x] **ET-G195.** Ahv sööb **banaani**.
- [x] **ET-G196.** Ootasime kontserdi **algust**.

_After any number above one_

- [x] **ET-G197.** Autol on neli **ratast**.
- [x] **ET-G198.** Nende peres on viis **last**.


### Case: ILLATIVE

_Going into a place or a container_

- [x] **ET-G199.** Kirjuta ülesanne **vihikusse**.
- [x] **ET-G200.** See trepp viib **keldrisse**.

_Going into a state or a stretch of time_

- [x] **ET-G201.** Buss sattus **õnnetusse**.
- [x] **ET-G202.** Lapsuke vajus **unne**.


### Case: ADESSIVE

_Position on a surface_

- [x] **ET-G203.** Söök on **laual**.
- [ ] **ET-G204.** Eva oli **diivanil** röötsakil.  
  FIX: (replace: 'röötsakil' (sprawled) is a rare colloquial word a learner will not know and it distracts from the adessive. Suggestion: 'Kass magab diivanil.' (bold diivanil).)  
  _check_

_Having something: the owner takes this ending_

- [x] **ET-G205.** **Mul** on oma maja.
- [x] **ET-G206.** **Tal** on suur perekond.

_When something happens_

- [x] **ET-G207.** Koosolek toimub **teisipäeval**.
- [x] **ET-G208.** Kontsert toimub **reedel**.


### Case: ABLATIVE

_Coming off a surface_

- [x] **ET-G209.** Korjasin **peenralt** lilli.
- [x] **ET-G210.** Kopli tramm sõitis **rööbastelt** maha.

_The person something is taken, bought or asked from_

- [x] **ET-G211.** Pead **vanematelt** luba paluma.
- [x] **ET-G212.** Ta sai **vanaemalt** pärandiks korteri.


### Case: TRANSLATIVE

_Turning into a state or a role_

- [x] **ET-G213.** Peske käed **puhtaks**.
- [x] **ET-G214.** Pliiats hakkab **nüriks** minema.

_What something is for_

- [x] **ET-G215.** Sain **kingituseks** tahvli šokolaadi.
- [x] **ET-G216.** **Lõunaks** oli kanafilee riisiga.

_A deadline: by when_

- [x] **ET-G217.** Töö valmib **neljapäevaks**.
- [x] **ET-G218.** Pean töö **homseks** lõpetama.


### Case: TERMINATIVE

_As far as a place_

- [x] **ET-G219.** Nad kõndisid edasi ja jõudsid **järveni**.
- [x] **ET-G220.** **Linnani** on poolteist kilomeetrit.

_Until a moment_

- [x] **ET-G221.** Ta töötab varahommikust **hilisõhtuni**.
- [x] **ET-G222.** Näitus jääb avatuks **sügiseni**.

_Up to an amount_

- [x] **ET-G223.** Arvud ühest **kümneni**.
- [x] **ET-G224.** Päeval tõuseb temperatuur viieteist **kraadini**.


### Case: ESSIVE

_Working as something_

- [x] **ET-G225.** Ta töötab **juuksurina**.
- [x] **ET-G226.** Ema töötab **arhitektina**.

_A role or a capacity you are in for now_

- [ ] **ET-G227.** Marie suri **vallalisena**.  
  FIX: (replace: 'Marie died unmarried' is grim and old-fashioned, and a death is not a role 'you are in for now'. Suggestion: 'Lapsena elasin maal.' (bold Lapsena) or 'Ta oli seal tõlgina.' (bold tõlgina).)  
  _check_
- [x] **ET-G228.** Majaelanikud pääsesid põlengust **tervena**.


### Case: ABESSIVE

_The absence of a thing_

- [x] **ET-G229.** Ilma **autota** pole supermarketisse mõtet minna.
- [x] **ET-G230.** Rasedus kulges **probleemideta**.

_Doing something without a tool, a person or permission_

- [x] **ET-G231.** Kasutasin **juhtmeta** kõrvaklappe.
- [x] **ET-G232.** Mees rääkis peaaegu **pausideta**.


### Case: COMITATIVE

_Together with somebody_

- [x] **ET-G233.** Käisin **sõbrannaga** kohvikus.
- [x] **ET-G234.** Fotol on Mari **tütrega**.

_The tool you did it with_

- [x] **ET-G235.** Laps mängib **palliga**.
- [x] **ET-G236.** Suppi süüakse **lusikaga**.

_How you got somewhere, like by bus_

- [x] **ET-G237.** Käin tööl **jalgrattaga**.
- [x] **ET-G238.** Sõitsin **trammiga** koju.


### Case: INESSIVE

_Position inside a place_

- [x] **ET-G239.** Käisin **meres** ujumas.
- [x] **ET-G240.** Süütasin **ahjus** tule.

_Being in a state, a language, or a month_

- [x] **ET-G241.** **Detsembris** on jõulud.
- [x] **ET-G242.** Oled elanud **vales**.


### Case: ELATIVE

_Coming out of a place_

- [x] **ET-G243.** Laev väljub **sadamast**.
- [ ] **ET-G244.** Me jäime **bussist** maha.  
  FIX: (replace: 'bussist maha jääma' means missing the bus, not coming out of it, so it contradicts the context. Suggestion: 'Ta astus bussist välja.' (bold bussist).)  
  _check_

_What something is made of_

- [x] **ET-G245.** **Odrast** saab karaskit.
- [x] **ET-G246.** Ta kannab **villast** kampsunit.

_What a text or a conversation is about_

- [x] **ET-G247.** **Millest** sa mõtled?
- [x] **ET-G248.** Saime teate isa **surmast**.


### Case: ALLATIVE

_Going onto a surface_

- [x] **ET-G249.** Pane raamat **lauale**.
- [x] **ET-G250.** Külaline istus **toolile**.

_The person something is given, said or sent to_

- [x] **ET-G251.** Tegin **lapsele** vanni.
- [x] **ET-G252.** Tüdruk andis **poisile** musu.


## Russian and Ukrainian: sentence openers (Lause algus)

From `lib/copy/i18n/openerSentences.ts`. Each template shown with one word, then the noun forms.


### Russian: templates

- [x] **OP-RU1.** Mulle meeldib … (I like …)  
  → Мне нравится телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU2.** Ma tahan … (I want …)  
  → Я хочу телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU3.** Mul on … (I have …)  
  → У меня есть телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU4.** Mul ei ole … (I don’t have …)  
  → У меня нет телефона.
- [x] **OP-RU5.** Ma vajan … (I need …)  
  → Мне нужен телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU6.** Ma otsin … (I’m looking for …)  
  → Я ищу телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU7.** Ma soovin … (I’d like to have …)  
  → Мне хотелось бы иметь телефон.
- [ ] **OP-RU8.** Ma tahaksin … (I would want …)  
  → Мне бы хотелось телефон.  
  FIX: Я бы хотел (хотела) телефон.  
  _check: Мне бы хотелось with a plain object noun sounds non-native; the personal form is what Russians say. The template then needs both gender forms written out, as elsewhere in the app._
- [x] **OP-RU9.** Ma ootan … (I’m waiting for …)  
  → Я жду автобус.
- [x] **OP-RU10.** Mulle maitseb … (The … tastes good to me.)  
  → Яблоко мне по вкусу.
- [x] **OP-RU11.** Laual on … (There is … on the table.)  
  → На столе есть книга.
- [x] **OP-RU12.** Meil on … (We have …)  
  → У нас есть телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU13.** Tal on … (He or she has …)  
  → У него или у неё есть телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU14.** Ma ei taha … (I don’t want …)  
  → Я не хочу телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU15.** Ma ei vaja … (I don’t need …)  
  → Мне не нужен телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU16.** Mul pole … (I haven’t got …)  
  → Нет у меня телефона.
- [x] **OP-RU17.** Meil ei ole … (We don’t have …)  
  → У нас нет телефона.
- [x] **OP-RU18.** Laual ei ole … (There is no … on the table.)  
  → На столе нет книги.
- [x] **OP-RU19.** Mulle ei meeldi … (I don’t like …)  
  → Мне не нравится телефон.
- [ ] **OP-RU20.** Ma tahtsin … (I wanted …)  
  → Мне хотелось телефон.  
  FIX: Я хотел (хотела) телефон.  
  _check: Мне хотелось телефон sounds non-native; a native says Я хотел(а). The template then needs both gender forms written out, as elsewhere in the app._
- [ ] **OP-RU21.** Ma ei tahtnud … (I didn’t want …)  
  → Мне не хотелось телефон.  
  FIX: Я не хотел (хотела) телефон.  
  _check: Мне не хотелось телефон sounds non-native; a native says Я не хотел(а). The template then needs both gender forms written out, as elsewhere in the app._
- [x] **OP-RU22.** Mul oli … (I had …)  
  → У меня был телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU23.** Mul ei olnud … (I didn’t have …)  
  → У меня не было телефона.
- [x] **OP-RU24.** Mulle meeldis … (I liked …)  
  → Мне нравился телефон.
- [x] **OP-RU25.** Mulle meeldivad … (I like …)  
  → Мне нравятся книги.
- [x] **OP-RU26.** Ma tahan … (I want …)  
  → Я хочу книги.
- [x] **OP-RU27.** Mul on … (I have …)  
  → У меня есть книги.
- [x] **OP-RU28.** Mul ei ole … (I don’t have …)  
  → У меня нет книг.
- [x] **OP-RU29.** Ma ei taha … (I don’t want …)  
  → Я не хочу книги.
- [x] **OP-RU30.** Mulle ei meeldi … (I don’t like …)  
  → Мне не нравятся книги.

  → собака, собаку, собаки; plural собаки, собак, собак (f)

  → кошка, кошку, кошки; plural кошки, кошек, кошек (f)

  → компьютер, компьютер, компьютера; plural компьютеры, компьютеры, компьютеров (m)
- [x] **OP-RU37.** bike  
  → велосипед, велосипед, велосипеда; plural велосипеды, велосипеды, велосипедов (m)
- [x] **OP-RU38.** pencil  
  → карандаш, карандаш, карандаша; plural карандаши, карандаши, карандашей (m)
- [x] **OP-RU39.** apple  
  → яблоко, яблоко, яблока; plural яблоки, яблоки, яблок (n)
- [x] **OP-RU40.** cake  
  → торт, торт, торта; plural торты, торты, тортов (m)
- [x] **OP-RU41.** bag  
  → сумка, сумку, сумки; plural сумки, сумки, сумок (f)
- [x] **OP-RU42.** ticket  
  → билет, билет, билета; plural билеты, билеты, билетов (m)
- [x] **OP-RU43.** banana  
  → банан, банан, банана; plural бананы, бананы, бананов (m)
- [x] **OP-RU44.** bus  
  → автобус, автобус, автобуса; plural автобусы, автобусы, автобусов (m)

### Ukrainian: templates

- [x] **OP-UK1.** Mulle meeldib … (I like …)  
  → Мені подобається телефон.
- [x] **OP-UK2.** Ma tahan … (I want …)  
  → Я хочу телефон.
- [x] **OP-UK3.** Mul on … (I have …)  
  → У мене є телефон.
- [ ] **OP-UK4.** Mul ei ole … (I don’t have …)  
  → У мене немає телефона.  
  FIX: У мене немає телефону.  
  _check: The standard and far more common genitive of телефон is телефону._
- [x] **OP-UK5.** Ma vajan … (I need …)  
  → Мені потрібен телефон.
- [x] **OP-UK6.** Ma otsin … (I’m looking for …)  
  → Я шукаю телефон.
- [x] **OP-UK7.** Ma soovin … (I’d like to have …)  
  → Мені хотілося б мати телефон.
- [ ] **OP-UK8.** Ma tahaksin … (I would want …)  
  → Мені б хотілося телефон.  
  FIX: Я б хотів (хотіла) телефон.  
  _check: Мені б хотілося with a plain object noun is non-standard (хотітися takes the genitive); the personal form is natural. The template then needs both gender forms written out, as elsewhere in the app._
- [x] **OP-UK9.** Ma ootan … (I’m waiting for …)  
  → Я чекаю на автобус.
- [x] **OP-UK10.** Mulle maitseb … (The … tastes good to me.)  
  → Яблуко мені до смаку.
- [x] **OP-UK11.** Laual on … (There is … on the table.)  
  → На столі є книжка.
- [x] **OP-UK12.** Meil on … (We have …)  
  → У нас є телефон.
- [x] **OP-UK13.** Tal on … (He or she has …)  
  → У нього чи в неї є телефон.
- [x] **OP-UK14.** Ma ei taha … (I don’t want …)  
  → Я не хочу телефон.
- [x] **OP-UK15.** Ma ei vaja … (I don’t need …)  
  → Мені не потрібен телефон.
- [ ] **OP-UK16.** Mul pole … (I haven’t got …)  
  → Нема в мене телефона.  
  FIX: Нема в мене телефону.  
  _check: The standard and far more common genitive of телефон is телефону._
- [ ] **OP-UK17.** Meil ei ole … (We don’t have …)  
  → У нас немає телефона.  
  FIX: У нас немає телефону.  
  _check: The standard and far more common genitive of телефон is телефону._
- [x] **OP-UK18.** Laual ei ole … (There is no … on the table.)  
  → На столі немає книжки.
- [x] **OP-UK19.** Mulle ei meeldi … (I don’t like …)  
  → Мені не подобається телефон.
- [ ] **OP-UK20.** Ma tahtsin … (I wanted …)  
  → Мені хотілося телефон.  
  FIX: Я хотів (хотіла) телефон.  
  _check: Мені хотілося телефон is non-standard; a native says Я хотів (хотіла). The template then needs both gender forms written out, as elsewhere in the app._
- [ ] **OP-UK21.** Ma ei tahtnud … (I didn’t want …)  
  → Мені не хотілося телефон.  
  FIX: Я не хотів (хотіла) телефон.  
  _check: Мені не хотілося телефон is non-standard; a native says Я не хотів (хотіла). The template then needs both gender forms written out, as elsewhere in the app._
- [x] **OP-UK22.** Mul oli … (I had …)

  FIX: У мене не було телефону.  
  _check: The standard and far more common genitive of телефон is телефону._

## Review summary

Every entry is either ticked `[x]` or has a `FIX:` line under it, with the reason in italics ("sure" is an error or the address switch, "check" is correct but not how a native would say it). 23,551 entries are ticked and 9,296 have a fix.

| Section | Fixed |
|---|---|
| ET-L, ET-P, ET-T | 0 (all right) |
| ET-S scene closings | 11 |
| ET-J jokes | 9 |
| ET-W public pages | 413 |
| ET-E brief for EKI | 12 |
| SL store listing | 29 |
| ET-G grammar examples | 17 (replacement suggested; the EKI sentence itself is not edited) |
| ET-B workbook | 42 (19 refuse, 23 keep with a corrected English) |
| OP openers | 11 |
| RU | 4,280 |
| UK | 4,472 |

Decisions applied throughout:

- Russian and Ukrainian address the learner with ты / ти everywhere: instructions, buttons, errors, emails, public and legal pages. Where ты would force a gendered past form, the line is rephrased, or both forms are written out (сделал (сделала), зробив (зробила)).
- вы / ви stays where it is not the learner being addressed: dialogue with strangers, staff and officials, glosses of Estonian teie forms, examiner questions, partner-interview questions, and "both of you" lines.
- The Estonian public pages use sina everywhere. The brief for EKI keeps teie, since it is a letter to an institution.
- The organizations (pilot) page now uses sina in Estonian, so its Russian and Ukrainian lines use ты / ти too.

Worth a look before applying:

- OP-RU8, 20, 21 and OP-UK8, 20, 21: "Мне хотелось телефон" is not natural, but the fix ("Я хотел (хотела) телефон") changes the template's shape, so it needs a change in `openerSentences.ts`, not just new text. The Ukrainian genitive is телефону, which also changes the noun row OP-UK33.
- ET-W592: the English "nothing they have learned goes with them" is ambiguous; the fix reads it as the learner losing nothing.
- ET-W312, 566, 567 and 660: the Estonian drafts drop the `{local}` slot; kept as drafted.
- Terms used two ways on the public pages: "deployment" is both juurutus and paigaldus, and "review" is both kordus and kordamine.
- A few Russian and Ukrainian lines add a note for Russian or Ukrainian speakers that the English lacks (for example "по-русски или по-английски"); they look deliberate and were left in.
- Nine Ukrainian ids had no Ukrainian line in this file; a fix written for them was dropped.
